 5fc2cc         vec3 processDeband(vec3 rgb_src) {
 5fc2f7             float blurFlag = 1.0;
 5fc319             float thr_ = 0.01;
 5fc338             float ditherFlag = 1.0;
 5fc35c             float dit_str = 0.8;
 5fc37d             float gauss_noise;
 5fc39c             vec3 finalColor;
 5fc3ba             // 
 5fc3d9             vec3 ref0_src = getRGB(ref0) * 255.0;
 5fc40b             vec3 ref1_src = getRGB(ref1) * 255.0;
 5fc43d             vec3 ref2_src = getRGB(ref2) * 255.0;
 5fc46f             vec3 ref3_src = getRGB(ref3) * 255.0;
 5fc4a1             // 
 5fc4ba             vec3 src0 = rgb_src * 255.0;
 5fc4e4             // 
 5fc524             vec3 avg = (ref0_src + ref1_src + ref2_src + ref3_src) / 4.0;
 5fc56e             vec3 diff = abs(src0 - avg);
 5fc598             // 
 5fc5ad blur
 5fc5c1             if (blurFlag > 0.5) {
 5fc5e3                 // 
 5fc627                 vec3 mask = step(thr_, diff); // 
 5fc65e diff >= thr_
 5fc68a                 if (ditherFlag > 0.5) { // dither
 5fc6c2                     // 
 5fc716                     float gauss_noise_r = generateGaussianNoise(v_texCoord, sysTime);//[-5.26, 5.26]
 5fc77b                     float gauss_noise_g = generateGaussianNoise(v_texCoord + vec2(0.37, 0.11), sysTime + 0.7);
 5fc7ea                     float gauss_noise_b = generateGaussianNoise(v_texCoord + vec2(0.73, 0.41), sysTime + 1.3);
 5fc859                     vec3 gauss_noise = vec3(gauss_noise_r, gauss_noise_g, gauss_noise_b);
 5fc8b4                     // 
 5fc8de                     vec3 wgt_noise = floor(gauss_noise * dit_str * (1.0 - src0 * 0.00390625) + 0.5);
 5fc944                     // 
 5fc961 clip
 5fc96c                     avg = av_clip_uintp2vec3(avg + wgt_noise, 8.0);
 5fc9b0                 }
 5fc9c3                 // 
 5fc9f5                 finalColor = mix(avg, src0, mask); // 
 5fca31 mask
 5fca4e mask
 5fca5f src0
 5fca64             }else {
 5fca78                 // blur
 5fca9f                 // 
 5fcadd                 vec3 mask0 = step(thr_, abs(src0 - ref0_src));// abs(src0 - ref0_src) >= thr_
 5fcb59                 vec3 mask1 = step(thr_, abs(src0 - ref1_src));
 5fcb98                 vec3 mask2 = step(thr_, abs(src0 - ref2_src));
 5fcbd7                 vec3 mask3 = step(thr_, abs(src0 - ref3_src));
 5fcc17                 // 
 5fcc64                 vec3 allBelowThreshold = vec3(
 5fcc93                     (mask0.r + mask1.r + mask2.r + mask3.r) < 0.5 ? 1.0 : 0.0,
 5fcce2                     (mask0.g + mask1.g + mask2.g + mask3.g) < 0.5 ? 1.0 : 0.0,
 5fcd31                     (mask0.b + mask1.b + mask2.b + mask3.b) < 0.5 ? 1.0 : 0.0
 5fcd7f                 );
 5fcd93                 finalColor = mix(avg, src0, 1.0 - allBelowThreshold); // 
 5fcde2 1.0 - allBelowThreshold
 5fce12 1.0 - allBelowThreshold
 5fce36 src0
 5fce3b             }
 5fce4a             // 
 5fce77 vec3
 5fce7c             return finalColor/255.0;
 5fcea1         }

 5fe482                 float direction = 2.0 * M_PI;
 5fe4b0                 float range = 16.0;
 5fe4d5                 // 
 5fe4ee ref 0-3
 5fe4ff                 float x = vTextureCoord.x * frameWidth;
 5fe537                 float y = vTextureCoord.y * frameHeight;
 5fe571                 // 
 5fe58a GLSL
 5fe59b                 float r = frand(vec2(x, y));
 5fe5c8                 float dir = direction < 0.0 ? -direction : r * direction;
 5fe612                 float dist = range < 0.0 ? -range : r * range;
 5fe652                 float x_pos = cos(dir) * dist;
 5fe681                 float y_pos = sin(dir) * dist;
 5fe6b1                 float invframeWidth = 1.0 / frameWidth;
 5fe6e9                 float invframeHeight = 1.0 / frameHeight;
 5fe724                 ref0 = vec2(
 5fe741                     clamp(x + x_pos, 0.0, frameWidth) * invframeWidth,
 5fe788                     clamp(y + y_pos, 0.0, frameHeight) * invframeHeight
 5fe7d0                 );
 5fe7e3                 ref1 = vec2(
 5fe800                     clamp(x + x_pos, 0.0, frameWidth) * invframeWidth,
 5fe847                     clamp(y - y_pos, 0.0, frameHeight) * invframeHeight
 5fe88f                 );
 5fe8a2                 ref2 = vec2(
 5fe8bf                     clamp(x - x_pos, 0.0, frameWidth) * invframeWidth,
 5fe906                     clamp(y - y_pos, 0.0, frameHeight) * invframeHeight
 5fe94e                 );
 5fe961                 ref3 = vec2(
 5fe97e                     clamp(x - x_pos, 0.0, frameWidth) * invframeWidth,
 5fe9c5                     clamp(y + y_pos, 0.0, frameHeight) * invframeHeight
 5fea0d                 );
 5fea20             }
 5fea2e         }
 5fea3a #extension GL_OES_EGL_image_external : require
 5fea6a #ifdef GL_ES
 5fea77 #ifdef GL_FRAGMENT_PRECISION_HIGH
 5fea99         precision highp float;
 5feab8 #else
 5feabe         precision mediump float;
 5feadf #endif
 5feae6 #endif
 5feaed         varying vec2 vTextureCoord;
 5feb11         uniform samplerExternalOES sTexture;
 5feb3e         uniform int alphaMode;
 5feb5e         //deband related
 5feb77         varying vec2 ref0;
 5feb92         varying vec2 ref1;
 5febad         varying vec2 ref2;
 5febc8         varying vec2 ref3;
 5febe4         uniform float sysTime;
 5fec03         uniform mediump int enableDeband;
 5fec2e         //8
 5fec61         vec3 av_clip_uintp2vec3(vec3 a, float p) {
 5fec94             float max_val = pow(2.0, p) - 1.0;
 5fecc3             return clamp(a, 0.0, max_val);
 5fecee         }
 5fecf9         // 
 5fed26         float random(vec3 seed) {
 5fed48             return fract(sin(dot(seed.xyz, vec3(12.9898, 78.233, 37.719))) * 1.61803398);
 5feda2         }
 5fedad         // 
 5feddd         float generateGaussianNoise(vec2 texCoord, float systime) {
 5fee21             const float M_PI = 3.14159265358979323846;
 5fee58             // 
 5feeb4             vec3 seed1 = vec3(texCoord * 100.0, systime );
 5feeef             vec3 seed2 = vec3(texCoord * 100.0, 2.0 * systime + 1000.0); // 
 5fef52             // 
 5fef80             float rand1 = random(seed1);
 5fefa9             float rand2 = random(seed2);
 5fefd3             // 
 5fefe8 Box-Muller
 5ff00b             //
 5ff022 0.000000000000001
 5ff036 1e-15
 5ff081             float gaussNoise = sqrt(-2.0 * log(rand1 + 0.000001)) * cos(2.0 * M_PI * rand2);
 5ff0df             return gaussNoise;
 5ff0fe         }
 5ff109         vec4 processDeband() {
 5ff128             float blurFlag = 1.0;
 5ff14a             float thr_ = 0.01;
 5ff169             float ditherFlag = 1.0;
 5ff18d             float dit_str = 0.8;
 5ff1ae             float gauss_noise;
 5ff1cd             vec3 finalColor;
 5ff1eb             // 
 5ff20a             vec3 ref0_src = texture2D(sTexture, ref0).rgb * 255.0;
 5ff24d             vec3 ref1_src = texture2D(sTexture, ref1).rgb * 255.0;
 5ff290             vec3 ref2_src = texture2D(sTexture, ref2).rgb * 255.0;
 5ff2d3             vec3 ref3_src = texture2D(sTexture, ref3).rgb * 255.0;
 5ff316             // 
 5ff32f             vec3 src0 = texture2D(sTexture, vTextureCoord).rgb * 255.0;
 5ff378             // 
 5ff3b8             vec3 avg = (ref0_src + ref1_src + ref2_src + ref3_src) / 4.0;
 5ff402             vec3 diff = abs(src0 - avg);
 5ff42c             // 
 5ff441 blur
 5ff455             if (blurFlag > 0.5) {
 5ff477                 // 
 5ff4bb                 vec3 mask = step(thr_, diff); // 
 5ff4f2 diff >= thr_
 5ff51e                 if (ditherFlag > 0.5) { // dither
 5ff556                     // 
 5ff5aa                     float gauss_noise_r = generateGaussianNoise(vTextureCoord, sysTime);//[-5.26, 5.26]
 5ff612                     float gauss_noise_g = generateGaussianNoise(vTextureCoord + vec2(0.37, 0.11), sysTime + 0.7);
 5ff684                     float gauss_noise_b = generateGaussianNoise(vTextureCoord + vec2(0.73, 0.41), sysTime + 1.3);
 5ff6f6                     vec3 gauss_noise = vec3(gauss_noise_r, gauss_noise_g, gauss_noise_b);
 5ff751                     // 
 5ff77b                     vec3 wgt_noise = floor(gauss_noise * dit_str * (1.0 - src0 * 0.00390625) + 0.5);
 5ff7e1                     // 
 5ff7fe clip
 5ff809                     avg = av_clip_uintp2vec3(avg + wgt_noise, 8.0);
 5ff84d                 }
 5ff860                 // 
 5ff892                 finalColor = mix(avg, src0, mask); // 
 5ff8ce mask
 5ff8eb mask
 5ff8fc src0
 5ff901             }else {
 5ff915                 // blur
 5ff93c                 // 
 5ff97a                 vec3 mask0 = step(thr_, abs(src0 - ref0_src));// abs(src0 - ref0_src) >= thr_
 5ff9f6                 vec3 mask1 = step(thr_, abs(src0 - ref1_src));
 5ffa35                 vec3 mask2 = step(thr_, abs(src0 - ref2_src));
 5ffa74                 vec3 mask3 = step(thr_, abs(src0 - ref3_src));
 5ffab4                 // 
 5ffb01                 vec3 allBelowThreshold = vec3(
 5ffb30                     (mask0.r + mask1.r + mask2.r + mask3.r) < 0.5 ? 1.0 : 0.0,
 5ffb7f                     (mask0.g + mask1.g + mask2.g + mask3.g) < 0.5 ? 1.0 : 0.0,
 5ffbce                     (mask0.b + mask1.b + mask2.b + mask3.b) < 0.5 ? 1.0 : 0.0
 5ffc1c                 );
 5ffc30                 finalColor = mix(avg, src0, 1.0 - allBelowThreshold); // 
 5ffc7f 1.0 - allBelowThreshold
 5ffcaf 1.0 - allBelowThreshold
 5ffcd3 src0
 5ffcd8             }
 5ffce7             // 
 5ffd14 vec4
 5ffd19             return vec4(finalColor / 255.0, 1.0);
 5ffd4b         }
 5ffd56         void main() {
 5ffd6c             if (alphaMode == 0) {
 5ffd8e                 if (enableDeband != 0) {
 5ffdb7                     gl_FragColor = processDeband();
 5ffdeb                 } else {
 5ffe04                     gl_FragColor = texture2D(sTexture, vTextureCoord);
 5ffe4b                 }
 5ffe5d             } else {
 5ffe72                 vec3 rgb;
 5ffe8c                 float alpha;
 5ffea9                 if (alphaMode == 1) {
 5ffecf                     if (vTextureCoord.x < 0.25 || vTextureCoord.x >= 0.75) {
 5fff1c                         rgb   = vec3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
 5fff51                         alpha = 0.0;
 5fff76                     } else {
 5fff93                         rgb   = texture2D(sTexture, vec2(vTextureCoord.x - 0.25, vTextureCoord.y)).rgb;
 5ffffb                         alpha = texture2D(sTexture, vec2(vTextureCoord.x + 0.25, vTextureCoord.y)).r;
 600061                     }
 600077                 } else if (alphaMode == 2) {
 6000a4                     if (vTextureCoord.x < 0.25 || vTextureCoord.x >= 0.75) {
 6000f1                         rgb   = vec3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
 600126                         alpha = 0.0;
 60014b                     } else {
 600168                         rgb   = texture2D(sTexture, vec2(vTextureCoord.x + 0.25, vTextureCoord.y)).rgb;
 6001d0                         alpha = texture2D(sTexture, vec2(vTextureCoord.x - 0.25, vTextureCoord.y)).r;
 600236                     }
 60024c                 } else if (alphaMode == 3) {
 600279                     if (vTextureCoord.y < 0.25 || vTextureCoord.y >= 0.75) {
 6002c6                         rgb   = vec3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
 6002fb                         alpha = 0.0;
 600320                     } else {
 60033d                         rgb   = texture2D(sTexture, vec2(vTextureCoord.x, vTextureCoord.y + 0.25)).rgb;
 6003a5                         alpha = texture2D(sTexture, vec2(vTextureCoord.x, vTextureCoord.y - 0.25)).r;
 60040b                     }
 600421                 } else if (alphaMode == 4) {
 60044e                     if (vTextureCoord.y < 0.25 || vTextureCoord.y >= 0.75) {
 60049b                         rgb   = vec3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
 6004d0                         alpha = 0.0;
 6004f5                     } else {
 600512                         rgb   = texture2D(sTexture, vec2(vTextureCoord.x, vTextureCoord.y - 0.25)).rgb;
 60057a                         alpha = texture2D(sTexture, vec2(vTextureCoord.x, vTextureCoord.y + 0.25)).r;
 6005e0                     }
 6005f6                 }
 600608                 gl_FragColor = vec4(rgb, alpha);
 600639             }
 600647         }

in vec2 v_bottomTextureCoord; in vec2 v_bottomLeftTextureCoord; in vec2 v_bottomRightTextureCoord; uniform sampler2D inputTexture; uniform float strength; out vec4 outColor; float MIN3(float a, float b, float c) { return min(min(a,b), c); } float MAX3(float a, float b, float c) { return max(max(a,b), c); } vec3 yuv2rgb(in vec3 yuv) { float y = yuv.x; float u = yuv.y; float v = yuv.z; return vec3(y + 1.0/0.877*v, y - 0.39393*u - 0.58081*v, y + 1.0/0.493*u); } vec3 rgb2yuv(in vec3 rgb) { float y = 0.299*rgb.r + 0.587*rgb.g + 0.114*rgb.b; return vec3(y, 0.493*(rgb.b-y), 0.877*(rgb.r-y)); } void main() { vec4 rgba_a = texture(inputTexture, v_topLeftTextureCoord); vec4 rgba_b = texture(inputTexture, v_topTextureCoord); vec4 rgba_c = texture(inputTexture, v_topRightTextureCoord); vec4 rgba_d = texture(inputTexture, v_leftTextureCoord); vec4 rgba_e = texture(inputTexture, v_texCoord); vec3 yuv_g = rgb2yuv(rgba_g.rgb); vec3 yuv_h = rgb2yuv(rgba_h.rgb); vec3 yuv_i = rgb2yuv(rgba_i.rgb); float a = yuv_a.r * 255.0; float b = yuv_b.r * 255.0; float c = yuv_c.r * 255.0; float d = yuv_d.r * 255.0; mn = mn + mn2; float mx = MAX3(MAX3( d, e, f), b, h); float mx2 = MAX3(MAX3(mx, a, c), g, i); mx = mx + mx2; vec4 dst_y = vec4(0.0, yuv_e.g, yuv_e.b, 0.0); if(mx != 0.0){ float amp = sqrt(clamp(min(mn, 511.0 - mx) / mx, 0.0, 1.0));
